# Memento System – Change Tracker

Workspace root: `/home/Feesh/Documents/GitHub/ultima-memento-modified`

This document tracks every file created or edited for the Memento Coin / Artefact Book / Memento Collector feature set, plus later custom-shard changes (such as the Magery-powered magic carpet speed rule). Update it whenever further changes are made.

## Working Rules — change tracking & backups

Every source change leaves two trails:

- **Back up first.** Copy the file *before* editing it into `Backups/` (git-ignored) as `<OriginalFileName>.<YYYY-MM-DD-HHMMSS>.bak` — e.g. `Backups/ResourceMods.cs.2026-11-15-135748.bak`. One backup per editing session, and the numbered entry names it (`Backed up as …`).
- **Record the change.** Add the next numbered entry to `## Change History (newest last)` (bold one-line summary, indented `-` detail bullets, compile result at the end) and update the `## Files Created` / `## Files Edited` tables plus the matching `## Current Behaviour` section when behaviour changes. The last entry is **233**, so the next entry is **234**.

Paths in the tables are relative to the `World` folder — `World/` in this repository, which mirrors the older standalone copy at `/home/Feesh/World` the tables were first written against.

| `Source/Scripts/Items/Books/ArtefactBook.cs` | C# item + gump | `ArtefactBook` item (displayed as "Tome of Wondrous Artifacts") and `ArtefactBookGump`. Lists every standard artefact (`ArtifactLevel.StandardArtefact`) from `ArtifactQuestList` with no in-book title, 16 per page, prev/next arrows and just the page number at the bottom. Clicking an artefact opens a detail page with the icon on the left page and the stat sheet on the right page (`ItemProps.ItemProperties`), plus a curated "Unique Power" description for artefacts with special code-driven abilities. Uses shared `MementoCoin.MementoHue`. `ArtefactBook.ArtefactEntry` carries `Name`, `TypeName` and `Layer`. |
| `Source/Scripts/Items/Misc/MementoCoin.cs` | C# item | `MementoCoin` stackable currency (graphic `0xEED`). Defines the shared `public const int MementoHue = 2141`. |
| `Source/Scripts/Mobiles/Civilized/Merchants/MementoCollector.cs` | C# NPC | `MementoCollector : BaseNPC` (invulnerable / untargetable). Pays Memento Coins for dropped artefacts, trades 75 coins for an `ArtefactBook`, has a Talk gump, random male/female + name, leather outfit, cloak/sash/boots, lit lantern, idle emotes, random artefact gear swaps and a random one-handed artefact weapon. |
| `Compile-Linux.sh` | Shell script (shortcut) | Linux shortcut in the `World` folder. Resolves the repo base folder (parent of `World`), runs `git pull` there, then `cd`s to `Source/Tools` and runs `compile-world-linux.sh` (Mono `mcs` → `WorldLinux.exe`). Aborts before compiling if the pull fails. Marked executable (`+x`). |
| `Compile-Windows.bat` | Batch script (shortcut) | Windows shortcut in the `World` folder. Resolves the repo base folder (parent of `World`) via `%~dp0..`, runs `git pull` there, then `pushd`s to `Source\Tools` and `call`s `compile-world-win.bat` (.NET 4.0 `csc` → `World.exe`). Aborts before compiling if the pull fails. |
| `Source/Scripts/Items/Technology/ZorkPad.cs` | C# item + gump | `ZorkPad : Item` — **named `Zork!`**, DataPad art (`0x27FB`/`0x27FC`) pinned to the shard's green hue `0x85D` (`ZorkPad.GreenHue`), `Technology`, weight 1, `Circle150` light. Dropped by **Exodus**. Double-click (within 4 tiles) opens `ZorkPadGump`, a large (740×780) black terminal with green phosphor text, scrolling transcript, typeable prompt + ENTER, that drives `ZorkGame`. |
| `Source/Scripts/Items/Technology/ZorkGame.cs` | C# engine | `ZorkGame` — a compact, playable Zork I "White House" text adventure: 11 rooms, a real verb parser, items (mailbox/leaflet, sword, brass lantern, rug/trap door, trophy case, rope), lamp-driven darkness + grue death, and per-player state keyed by mobile serial. |
| `Source/Scripts/Items/Misc/FragmentOfInfinity.cs` | C# item + drop rule | `FragmentOfInfinity : Item` — **"Fragment of Infinity"**, graphic `7964`, hue `1266`, weight 1. Single-use: double-click in the backpack → target an artefact you are wearing or carrying → adds **10 enchantment points** to the matching pool (`IGiftable.Points` for gift artefacts, `ILevelable.Points` for legendary leveling artefacts) and the fragment is consumed. A non-artefact target answers **"This only works on artefacts."** and the fragment is kept. The tooltip line "Adds 10 points of enchantment to any artefact" is drawn beneath the name in hue `71e26c` (`ColorText3`/`ColorHue3`). Also holds `FragmentOfInfinityDrops`: a flat **10%** corpse drop from the 23 named bosses, all 25 Magical Prison bosses and every champion spawn champion; since #219 its carrier test delegates to `MobileUtilities.IsElite`, the same registry that drives the **"(elite)"** name tag. |
| `Source/Scripts/System/Misc/VendorValue.cs` | C# tooltip hook | `VendorValue` — appends a **Worth** line to every item tooltip showing what a merchant would pay for the item, using the vendor sell formula (`GenericSellInfo.GetSellPriceFor`) on a global `ItemSalesInfo` sell table, with the viewer's Mercantile as the barter value. Mirrors `BaseVendor.TryGetSellState`'s refusals and rebuilds the OPL per player by re-registering the property-query handlers (`0xD6` in the normal **and** 6017 tables, plus `0xBF/0x10`). Also registers the player command `[VendorValues` to toggle the line and exposes `HideLegacyWorthText` for the core hook that suppresses the old relic `ColorText3` worth header. Unidentified relics always show the old 5–25 gold estimate; `NotIdentified` loot wrappers show the wrapped item's base type/material price (modifiers ignored). |
| `Source/Scripts/System/Misc/ItemRank.cs` | C# tooltip hook | `ItemRank` — Diablo-style colour tier (Common → Primal, with **Artefact** as the top tier `FFC000`), drawn in the same tooltip slot the artefact marker uses. The lower tiers come from the shard's weighted strength budget (`ItemInformation.AddUpBenefits` with the base price removed, plus material and quality; self repair credited at most one point); artefacts are assigned the top tier directly and keep their marker. Unidentified loot wrappers (`NotIdentified`) unwrap to their contained item, so the tier line and coloured name still show while the header text stays the generic "unknown item". Invoked from `Item.AddNameProperties` through a reflection hook. |
| `Source/Scripts/System/Commands/LootTierCommands.cs` | C# dev commands | `[TierCommon` … `[TierPrimal` (GameMaster) spawn one random equipment item built to that tier; `[TierAll` spawns one of each and `[TierItem <tier>` takes the name. Rolls attributes until `ItemRank.GetPower` lands in the tier band (floors read from `ItemRank`). Also registers the player command `[LootTiers` to toggle the cosmetic display. |
| `Documentation/Generate-CodebaseMap.py` | Python tool | Regenerates `Memento_Codebase_Map.md` from the current tree: parses every `.cs` file for type/base/commands/summary/AI/name literals, emits repository layout, cross-cutting systems, folder index and per-file catalog. Run with `python3 World/Documentation/Generate-CodebaseMap.py`. |
| `Source/Scripts/Items/Misc/DungeonChallengeOrb.cs` | C# item + static system | `DungeonChallengeOrb` consumable (graphic `0x122B`) + `DungeonChallengeBoost` static region registry. Use inside a dungeon to respawn its region XML spawns and in-region `PremiumSpawner`/`Spawner` items, then add +1 heat for `BoostMinutes` (default 15, capped 240) to that region and its child regions. |
| `Source/Scripts/System/Commands/DungeonChallengeCommands.cs` | C# command | GM `[DungeonOrb` command; puts a `DungeonChallengeOrb` in the caller's backpack. |
| `Source/Scripts/Mobiles/Civilized/Comrades/HenchmanCustomizeGump.cs` | C# gumps | `HenchmanCustomizeGump` / `HenchmanCustomizeNameGump` / `HenchmanCustomizeHairGump` / `HenchmanCustomizeBeardGump` / `HenchmanCustomizeGenderGump` / `HenchmanCustomizeSkinGump` — dark vendor-style customization UI with immediate live updates and deed persistence. Name/title 32-char HTML-sanitized; hair/beard vendor styles + 48 natural hues; 57 human skin hues; gender body switch with female-only gear guard, and the henchman is re-sent to nearby clients after a switch so their paperdoll rebuilds its cached gender (`ResendBodyToClients`, see #227). The henchman's paperdoll is opened with the customization and re-opened after a name, gender or skin change (`ShowPaperdoll`, see #228). |

| `Source/Scripts/Items/Houses/Remodeling/LawnTarget.cs` | Lawn/yard tools failed for houses inside named/custom regions (e.g. **the Ranger Outpost**): `ValidatePlacement` required the target's region to be the default region or pass `AllowHousing`, and `OutDoorRegion.AllowHousing` only allows the outpost for players with Camping/Tracking base ≥ 90. Added a short-circuit so lawn items can be placed on the player's own house land (`m_House.IsCoOwner(from)`) regardless of the surrounding region, and aligned the ownership check from `IsOwner` to `IsCoOwner` (matching `LawnTools.InHouse` and `InteriorDecorator`). Also replaced the player-relative placement box (Front/Back/Left/Right from the player's spot) with a house-footprint-relative bound (footprint via `house.Area` + the same 15-tile yard margin) through `IsWithinHouseLand`, so exact player position no longer matters — only that the item lands within the house's land. Backed up as `Backups/LawnTarget.cs.2026-11-08-092723.bak` (region fix) and `Backups/LawnTarget.cs.2026-11-08-094152.bak` (house-relative bounds). **Final:** per request, `ValidatePlacement` now ignores region entirely and drops the distance check — placement succeeds anywhere as long as the player is standing in a house they own/co-own (`FindHouseAt` + `IsCoOwner`); `IsWithinHouseLand` removed. Backed up as `Backups/LawnTarget.cs.2026-11-08-100718.bak`. **Range restored:** re-added `IsWithinHouseLand` using `house.Components` + `Remodeling` margin (15) so placement is limited to footprint+15; region still ignored. Backed up as `Backups/LawnTarget.cs.2026-11-08-102748.bak`. |

| `Source/Scripts/Items/Magical/Artifacts/Clothing/Outer/Artifact_HoodedShroudOfShadows.cs` | Now displayed as **"Hooded Shroud of Shadows"** and built on the hooded shroud art `0x2684` (`[Flipable( 0x2684, 0x2683 )]`), the same base as `GiftHoodedShroudOfShadows`, instead of the plain robe art `0x2B69`. Skill bonuses, `ArtifactLevel`, hue `0x455`, weight and loot type are unchanged. Serialisation version moved to 2 and `Deserialize` migrates saved copies (plain-robe art → `0x2684`, old name → new name), leaving transmogrified and clothing-placeholder items' current art alone. | `Source/System/Item.cs` | `AddNameProperties` now calls `InvokeItemTier( this, list )` immediately after the `ArtifactLevel` switch for every item, so equipment — including artefacts, which receive the top `Artefact` tier in addition to their marker — gets its tier line in exactly the slot the artefact marker occupies. The hook resolves `Server.ItemRank.AppendTier( Item, ObjectPropertyList )` through `ScriptCompiler.FindTypeByFullName`, caches it as a delegate and guards the call, so the core carries no script reference and simply draws no line if the script is absent or throws. `AddNameProperty` also calls `InvokeColoredName`, letting `Server.ItemRank` supply the rarity-coloured name, and `AddNameProperties` calls `InvokeNameHue` so `ColorText1/2` header lines (loot quivers) use the same tier hue. `AddNameProperties` also calls `InvokeSuppressColorText3` before printing the `1072173` header line; the hook resolves `Server.VendorValue.HideLegacyWorthText`, which hides the legacy relic `"Worth N Gold"` text without touching the saved `ColorText3` value. A fifth hook, `InvokeLabelHue`, resolves `Server.ItemRank.GetLabelHue` and supplies the hue for single-click/overhead labels (`OnSingleClick`, `OnAosSingleClick`, `LabelTo*`); it returns `0x3B2` unless the script overrides it (ground artefacts use `0x0B54` while Show Loot Tiers is on). Backed up as `Backups/Item.cs.2026-11-21-093036.bak` (tier hook), `Backups/Item.cs.2026-11-21-113420.bak` (name hook), `Backups/Item.cs.2026-11-21-114903.bak` (header hue hook), `Backups/Item.cs.2026-11-21-124608.bak` (legacy worth hook) and `Backups/Item.cs.2026-11-21-145758.bak` (label hue hook). |

Backed up as `Backups/BaseWeapon.cs.2026-11-25-220713.bak`. |

| `Source/Scripts/Mobiles/Civilized/Comrades/HenchmanItem.cs` | Equipment persistence for the fighter/archer/wizard deeds: new `List<Item> HenchEquipment` and `bool HenchGearMade`, serialization version 0 → 1, saved-gear restore on summon, and orphan-only cleanup in `OnDelete`. Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanItem.cs.2026-11-24-184536.bak`. **Follow-up:** `OnDoubleClick` clamps the copied skill value to a minimum of **31** before building any henchman, so every class skill has a floor even when the owner's skills are lower. Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanItem.cs.2026-11-24-204920.bak`. **Follow-up (#223):** after the saved gear is restored and the resistances normalised, `OnDoubleClick` calls `HenchmanFunctions.RefreshEquipmentPools( friend, true )` so a freshly summoned fighter/archer/wizard starts at the full equipment-boosted hit point, stamina and mana maxima. Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanItem.cs.2026-11-25-135410.bak`. **Follow-up (#229):** the `HenchmanFunctions.CheckDurability` call was removed from `OnDoubleClick` with the rest of the durability warnings. | `Source/Scripts/Mobiles/Civilized/Comrades/HenchmanFunctions.cs` | Added the equipment stash/restore, paperdoll-access, validation, default-gear and durability helpers; `DressUp` now post-processes every default piece to movable/regular/StrReq 10/`henchman's armor` or `henchman's weapon`; `OnDead` and the low-fame dismissal path stash the gear before the deed is cleared; bandage and accepted gold/treasure gifts run the durability check; legacy immovable+blessed defaults are migrated when first stashed. See #220. Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanFunctions.cs.2026-11-24-184536.bak`. **Follow-up:** `ValidateHenchmanEquip` now checks the item's Strength requirement against the henchman and says the visible **“I'm not strong enough to use that.”** before the core `CanEquip` would otherwise send its message to the clientless henchman. Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanFunctions.cs.2026-11-24-204920.bak`. **Follow-up (#222):** added `UsesWeaponDamageTypes` (fighter/archer/wizard take their attack's elemental split from the weapon) and `CanSpendHenchmanPoints` + `HenchmanPointsRefused` (only the henchman's owner, or a GM, may spend enchantment points on gear the henchman is carrying). Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanFunctions.cs.2026-11-24-224923.bak`. **Follow-up (#223):** added `GetEquipmentPoolMax` (adds `AosAttributes.BonusHits/BonusStam/BonusMana` to a seeded creature maximum, clamped 1..65000) and `RefreshEquipmentPools` (clamps or fills the current pools and re-arms the regen timers); `OnHenchmanEquipped`/`OnHenchmanItemRemoved` call it, and `EquipStoredGear` now runs `item.OnEquip( henchman )` before its direct `AddItem` for wizard staves and for the `EquipItem` fallback so weapon/armour stat mods apply. Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanFunctions.cs.2026-11-25-135410.bak`. **Follow-up (#229):** added `IgnoresDurability( Mobile )` (fighter/archer/wizard/monster henchmen never wear the gear they hold) and removed `CheckDurability` plus its summon/bandage/gold call sites, so the “looking worn” and “in danger of breaking” lines no longer exist. Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanFunctions.cs.2026-11-25-220713.bak`. **Follow-up (#230):** the `HenchmanWizard` branch of `ValidateHenchmanEquip` now refuses bows/crossbows (`IsBowOrCrossbow`) and throwing gloves (`IThrowingGloves`) with “I have no idea how to handle such a weapon.” before the spell-channelling test, so a channelling bow is no longer accepted. Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanFunctions.cs.2026-11-25-222528.bak`. **Follow-up (#231):** added `IsHarpoon` (the `IHarpoon` marker) to all three class rules, so `Harpoon`/`GiftHarpoon`/`LevelHarpoon` are refused by fighters (“Give me a proper melee weapon.”), archers (“Give me a bow or crossbow.”) and mages (“I have no idea how to handle such a weapon.”, spell channelling or not). Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanFunctions.cs.2026-11-25-234641.bak`. **Follow-up (#232):** added `IsBlowgun` (`Fukiya` and its `FukiyaDarts` ammo) to the same three rules, so a blowgun is refused by fighters (“Give me a proper melee weapon.”), archers (“Give me a bow or crossbow.”) and mages (“I have no idea how to handle such a weapon.”, spell channelling or not). Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanFunctions.cs.2026-11-25-235306.bak`. **Follow-up (#233):** added the `m_HandToHandWarning` flag (set when a mage accepts a `BaseWeapon` whose `Skill` is not `Marksmanship`, cleared with `m_MeditationWarning` at the top of every validation and on the strength refusal) and `AnnounceHandToHandWarning`, which speaks “I'll carry this, but don't expect me to fight hand-to-hand.” after the equip succeeds. Backed up as `Backups/HenchmanFunctions.cs.2026-11-26-002031.bak`. |
